==============================================================================
SECTION 4: STATISTICAL UNCERTAINTY AND LIMITATIONS
==============================================================================

BOOTSTRAP
- Paired block bootstrap over session_key, n_iter=400, seed=42.
- Tag and baseline are sampled with the SAME session resampling.
- CI95 is empirical quantile [0.025, 0.975].
- Sessions with no tag or no baseline observations are skipped per cell.

DEPENDENCE
- Windows overlap: H6 and H12 and H24 share bars.
- N_eff is NOT exactly N/H. Real autocorrelation is stronger.
- We do NOT use classical binomial CI as definitive proof.

MULTIPLE COMPARISONS
- Approx number of comparisons in this report: 864
- Bonferroni threshold for p=0.05 would be about 5.79e-05
- We do NOT filter or rank by significance. No 'best' level, tag, or horizon is selected.

FORMULATION RULES
- If CI95 includes zero, we say the effect could NOT be distinguished from zero
  by this method. We do NOT say the effect disappears.
- We do NOT claim that any reach level covers costs.
- We do NOT claim that mean reversion is proven.
- We do NOT compute synthetic PnL.

==============================================================================
SECTION 5: LIMITATIONS REGARDING COSTS
==============================================================================

- Cost reference 0.20% is the MODEL value from main.py:
    BACKTEST_COMMISSION_RATE = 0.0005 per side
    BACKTEST_SLIPPAGE_RATE   = 0.0005 per side
    round-trip reference     = 0.20%
- This value is a MODEL reference, not a measured per-trade cost.
- A reach probability is an EVENT probability, not a trade return.
- Reach level >= 0.20% does NOT mean the level 'covers costs'.
- No entry price, no exit price, no fill price, no spread, no queue model.
- No realized PnL is computed or implied anywhere in this report.

==============================================================================
SECTION 6: DATA REQUIRED FOR ACTUAL POST-COST PnL
==============================================================================

To evaluate realized post-cost PnL, the following are missing and
are NOT invented here:

  1. Entry rule (close T0 vs next-open T0+1 vs limit vs market).
  2. Exit rule (time-stop, target-hit, structure-stop, trailing, mix).
  3. Execution prices: bid/ask, spread at T0, queue position, market impact.
  4. Commission schedule: broker + exchange + clearing.
  5. Realized spread data (not recoverable from OHLCV).
  6. Slippage model calibrated on real fills.
  7. In-bar conflict rule: when a single 5M bar touches both targets,
     which is considered first? (path within bar is unknown).

STATUS: only event/touch probabilities have been explored.
        Post-cost PnL cannot be evaluated from this dataset alone.

NO ORDERS WERE SENT. NO TRADING ACTION WAS PERFORMED.
==============================================================================
